The findings identify professional editing style as a key confounding variable in AI detector outputs, rather than establishing a full separation of text origin from linguistic style, raising concerns about fairness and reliability in academic settings.

</details>


### [149] [Graph-Guided Selective Unlearning for Language Models: Controlling Support Routes Beyond Forget Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26743)
*Waqas Khan,Tabinda Sarwar,Jingyue Cong,Xun Yi,Estrid He*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GRAPHSU，通过构建加权支持路径图并传播删除压力，扩展机器遗忘范围至邻接样本，显著降低软泄漏率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法仅处理显式指定的遗忘种子，但目标知识可通过释义、别名或相邻训练样本恢复，导致删除不彻底。

Method: 构建加权支持-路径图，沿图传播删除压力，对高风险邻居施加分级遗忘强度，实现结构化扩展删除。

Result: 在TOFU与PISTOL基准上，使用GPT-2 Medium和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct，软泄漏率降低最多49.5个百分点，优于种子对照。

Conclusion: 企业级有效遗忘需控制支持路径而非仅删除种子，强调图引导的结构化遗忘必要性。

Abstract: Enterprises fine-tune language models on proprietary data that may later require removal due to privacy, contractual, or compliance obligations. Selective unlearning removes requested knowledge while preserving model utility, offering a practical alternative to full retraining, but existing methods treat the explicitly identified forget examples as the complete deletion scope. This is insufficient when target knowledge remains recoverable through paraphrases, aliases, or neighboring training examples. We propose GRAPHSU, a graph-guided controller that expands the deletion scope beyond forget seeds by constructing a weighted support-route graph, propagating deletion pressure through it, and applying graded forgetting strengths to high-risk neighbors. On the Task of Fictitious Unlearning (TOFU), a synthetic author-profile question-answering benchmark, and PISTOL, a structural-unlearning benchmark built around interconnected factual samples, with GPT-2 Medium and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, GRAPHSU achieves the lowest utility-feasible soft leakage across all deletion settings, reducing leakage by up to 49.5 percentage points over a matched seed-only baseline, demonstrating that effective enterprise unlearning requires controlling support routes, not just forget seeds.

</details>


### [150] [AgentFold: Closed-Loop Agentic Search for Protein Folding Model Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26747)
*Mingquan Liu,Jiangyu Chen,Hanqun Cao,Xujun Zhang,Pengsen Ma,Xiangru Tang,Shuting Jin,Zhuo Yang,Tianfan Fu,Fang Wu,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AgentFold多智能体框架，在蛋白质折叠任务中自动搜索代码级修改以改进模型，超越基线并总结出稳定改进的设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学LLM智能体在文献推理、工具使用和实验规划上有进展，但能否通过可执行代码更改和昂贵计算验证自主改进大型紧耦合科学ML系统仍不明确，本文以蛋白质折叠为测试场景进行研究。

Method: 多智能体闭环搜索框架，从ESMFold出发，生成假设、实现和调试代码修改、评估变体、分析结果，并存储成功和失败干预；采用MCTS风格策略在评分高的搜索分支间分配计算资源。

Result: 在2000多行代码的工程规模代码库上，约探索80个变体，使用5000 GPU小时和1.7亿LLM令牌；匹配预算下，lDDT比独立Codex提案提高7.5%，优于随机搜索对照。

Conclusion: AgentFold能自主改进复杂科学ML系统，且干预轨迹揭示反复出现的设计规律：早期软可学习先验和门控精炼带来稳定收益，而直接几何扰动和几何条件反馈常导致训练不稳定。

Abstract: Scientific LLM agents have shown promise in literature reasoning, tool use, and experiment planning, but it remains unclear whether they can autonomously improve large, tightly coupled scientific machine-learning systems through executable code changes and computationally expensive validation. We study this question in protein folding, where progress requires coordinated architectural modifications, multi-objective evaluation, and domain-aware interpretation. We present AgentFold, a multi-agent framework that formulates folding-model development as a closed-loop search over executable code variants. Starting from ESMFold, AgentFold proposes hypotheses, implements and debugs code-level modifications, evaluates model variants, analyzes experimental outcomes, and stores both successful and failed interventions in structured memory. An MCTS-style policy allocates computational resources across high-scoring search branches. On an engineering-scale protein-folding codebase comprising more than 2,000 lines of code, AgentFold explores approximately 80 model variants using approximately 5,000 GPU-hours and 170 million LLM tokens. Under a matched computational budget, AgentFold improves the best lDDT by 7.5% over independent Codex proposals and outperforms a random-search control. Beyond model improvement, the resulting intervention traces reveal recurring empirical design patterns: stable gains tend to arise from early, soft, learnable priors and gated refinement, whereas direct geometric perturbations and geometry-conditioned feedback often destabilize training. The code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/lmqfly/AgentFold.

</details>


### [151] [Performance Foundations of Parallel & Distributed Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27046)
*Maciej Besta,Leonard Schmidt,Lara Nonino,Robert Gerstenberger,Pierre Pang,Patrik Okanovic,Ales Kubicek,Tiancheng Chen,Baraq Lipshitz,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了RLVR等RL后训练范式在LLM推理增强中的计算挑战，提出了面向RL-for-LLM的并行计算分类法，并给出了构建高性能、可扩展且成本有效的推理语言模型（RLM）的实践指南与开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLM（如DeepSeek-R1、o3、Kimi k1.5）虽显著提升推理能力，但其训练需数百万GPU小时且依赖多模型紧耦合流水线，远超传统监督训练的计算规模，成为并行与分布式系统瓶颈，亟需系统化分析计算与并行策略。

Method: 首先系统化梳理RL-for-LLM范式，从计算视角分析PPO、GRPO及其变体；其次建立涵盖数据、张量、流水线、序列、上下文、专家并行等传统技术，以及解耦放置、阶段融合、混合并行、异步执行等新颖多模型并行策略的分类法，并利用work-depth模型保证理论严谨性与可移植性。

Result: 提出了针对RLM训练的并行与优化技术分类体系，分析了现有RLM框架的优缺点，并提炼出可操作的设计准则，识别出若干尚未解决的研究空白。

Conclusion: RLM训练需算法与系统协同设计；通过系统化并行策略和成本感知优化，可在保持高性能的同时实现可扩展性和经济性，未来应聚焦混合并行执行与异步流水线的进一步优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and other RL-style post-training paradigms have been used for aligning large language models (LLMs) with reasoning standards. The resulting recent Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) such as DeepSeek-R1, o3, and Kimi k1.5 show that such RL-style post-training ("RL-for-LLMs") can substantially improve chain-of-thought reasoning, long-horizon planning, and self-correction. However, the computational footprint of these systems is massive: state-of-the-art RLM training requires millions of GPU-hours and tightly coupled multi-model pipelines that stress modern hardware far beyond classical supervised LLM training. This makes RLM training as much a parallel and distributed systems problem as an algorithmic one. In this work, to facilitate developing RLMs that are simultaneously high-performance, scalable, and cost-effective, we first systematize the RL-for-LLM paradigm and provide a compute-centric analysis of prominent post-training algorithmic frameworks: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), as well as their variants. Second, we develop a taxonomy of intra- and inter-model parallelism strategies for RL-for-LLMs, covering both traditional techniques (data, tensor, pipeline, sequence, context, and expert parallelism) as well as novel forms of parallelism and optimization techniques for multi-model RLM training, for example disaggregated placement, stage fusion, hybrid parallelism, and asynchronous execution. We harness the work-depth model of parallel computing to make our taxonomy and its insights rigorous and portable. Finally, we analyze existing RLM frameworks and we distill practical guidelines and outline open research directions for building scalable, fast, and cost-effective RLMs.

</details>


### [152] [DEEPCHART: How Far are LLMs from Faithful Data-Science Chart Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26757)
*Jiahui tang,Kuicai Dong,Dexun Li,Hongchao Gu,Haocheng Yu,Wei Han,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个专家标注的图表生成基准 DEEPCHART，包含 1482 个真实场景实例，按提取-推理-可视化管线分阶段评估，发现现代 LLM 生成的图表虽视觉合理但常隐含数据级幻觉，且仅扩大上下文窗口不足以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 能生成视觉上合理且遵循指令的图表，但在长文本、嘈杂和多模态上下文中，数据层面的幻觉难以检测，缺乏系统性基准来衡量这一差距。

Method: 构建 DEEPCHART 基准，基于真实科学论文、财务文件和生态报告，将图表生成分解为提取-推理-可视化三级流水线，对源数据提取、派生数据推理和图表渲染分别进行分阶段评估。

Result: 实验表明，最先进模型生成的图表在视觉上具有说服力，但常隐藏数据级幻觉，提取和推理错误在长文本和多模态现实场景中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 更大的上下文窗口不足以确保忠实图表生成，还需要可靠的证据提取和定量推理能力，且该基准及资源已公开。

Abstract: Faithful chart generation in real-world data-science workflows requires grounding visualizations in scattered evidence, computing chart-ready quantities, and rendering them accurately. Modern LLMs can produce visually plausible, instruction-compliant charts, yet data-level hallucinations remain difficult to detect in long, noisy, and multimodal contexts. To measure this gap, we introduce DEEPCHART, an expert-annotated benchmark of 1,482 task-conditioned chart-generation instances drawn from real-world scientific papers, financial filings, and ecosystem reports. DEEPCHART formulates chart generation as an Extract--Reason--Visualize pipeline and evaluates source-data extraction, derived-data reasoning, and chart rendering stage by stage. Experiments with state-of-the-art models show that visually plausible charts often conceal data-level hallucinations, with extraction and reasoning errors common in realistic long and multimodal settings. These findings suggest that larger context windows alone are insufficient; faithful chart generation also requires reliable evidence extraction and quantitative reasoning before rendering. Our benchmark and associated resources are available at https://github.com/tangdouer1005/DeepChart.

</details>


### [153] [Decoupling Planning and Control for Instructable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26788)
*Zineng Tang,Kelsey R. Allen,Sjoerd van Steenkiste,Ishita Dasgupta,Alane Suhr*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Instruct-to-Act系统，将VLM规划器与世界模型控制器解耦结合，通过行为克隆与语言重标注训练控制器，在7个具身环境中优于基线，支持高频控制与VLM可替换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLM擅长高层规划但难以生成低延迟动作，世界模型控制器擅长快速控制但缺乏开放任务指导，两者互补需结合以提升具身智能体在陌生环境中的可靠性。

Method: 训练世界模型控制器在稀疏高层文本指令下高频自主行动；用合成指令重标注控制器轨迹片段，联合优化行为克隆、奖励最大化和世界建模目标，实现语言可指令化。

Result: 在7个具身环境（含3个多智能体）中，解耦方法一致优于仅控制器和直接VLM生成动作变体，保持快速控制，可替换不同VLM规划器且无需微调，在6/7任务上与强基线竞争。

Conclusion: 验证了将VLM规划与低级控制解耦的有效性，为构建灵活、快速且可扩展的具身决策系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [154] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种面向有向图学习的非对称谱认证、敏感性分析与控制方法，无需对称性、非负性或锥保持假设，并通过数值实验验证其在有向学习场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习产生非对称传播算子，传统谱分析工具依赖对称性或锥保持，难以适应训练过程中的可微参数优化，且对边方向的预测信息缺乏控制手段。

Method: 基于广义铅笔的两侧锥Rayleigh框架，发展可计算的锥上下界用于后验包络，引入光滑软最小/最大代理保持严格单侧界且可微；利用右、左模态导数公式推导一阶图干预，实现自适应谱控制。

Result: 在正象限设置下实现了可计算的锥级包络；在Cora有向引文网络上，通过自适应重算右左敏感性，在累积边权削减预算0.5%内将主导谱级降低约21.5%，且测试精度不变；实验显示符号化非对称扰动引发内部特征对向边界互补准对转变，对称化会丢失边方向携带的预测信息。

Conclusion: 该方法扩展了谱工具到非对称、非锥保持的有向图学习场景，提供了严格的误差界限和可微优化路径，并展示了有向性在预测信息中的不可替代性，为自适应谱控制提供了理论基础和实践验证。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [155] [SymbolLKG: Towards Verifiable Logical Reasoning via Logical Knowledge Graph and Symbolic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26836)
*Haizhao Fan,Yuchi Xiong,Jize Wang,Xinping Guan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号架构，将逻辑知识图谱与动态求解器路由结合，以提升大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中的准确性和可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在严格多步推理中常出现幻觉和不一致，CoT缺乏验证机制，标准RAG难以处理复杂结构依赖，亟需一种能显式建模逻辑依赖并动态选择推理引擎的方法。

Method: 构建本体驱动的逻辑知识图谱，将逻辑规则和约束作为一等拓扑节点，显式建模文本依赖；设计逻辑路由器，结合拓扑感知的混合检索机制，动态将任务分派至最优符号引擎。

Result: 在逻辑推理基准上显著优于先进的提示方法和RAG基线，实现更高准确率与可验证的推理路径。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥补LLM在逻辑推理上的不足，为可解释、可靠的多步推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in natural language understanding, yet they struggle with strict multi-step reasoning, frequently suffering from hallucinations and inconsistency. Existing solutions like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) lack rigorous verification mechanisms, while standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often misses the complex, structural dependencies inherent in logical tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic architecture that integrates a Logical Knowledge Graph (LKG) with dynamic solver routing. Specifically, we introduce an ontology-based LKG that treats logical rules and constraints as first-class topological nodes, enabling explicit modeling of dependencies extracted from text. We further design a Logic Router to dynamically dispatch tasks to the optimal symbolic engine, which is supported by a topology-aware hybrid retrieval mechanism. Experimental results on logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prompting and RAG baselines, delivering higher accuracy and verifiable reasoning paths.

</details>


### [156] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LiveSim框架，通过可编辑行为假设与轨迹验证，建模直播生态中用户动态行为演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟器依赖静态用户画像，无法适应直播等社交密集环境中交互动态对行为的持续塑造。

Method: 将用户表示为可编辑行为假设，利用模拟与真实轨迹差异提取环境行为模式，存入集体行为记忆以提升保真度。

Result: 在真实直播风控数据上，用户行为保真度提升，并支持风险演化与平台干预的生态级分析。

Conclusion: LiveSim有效捕捉环境塑造效应，为动态社会生态模拟提供稳健方案。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [157] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RSS最少测量与高斯过程建模的低功耗定位方法，实现38mg轻量接收器在300m范围、15m精度及亚毫瓦功耗下的移动追踪，并应用于大黄蜂回巢轨迹分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低功耗定位依赖大量RSS测量以推断AoA，受限于RSS测量数量带来的功耗和距离瓶颈，难以满足超轻型移动设备（如昆虫）的追踪需求。

Method: 利用旋转高增益发射机，用最少RSS测量通过概率建模推断AoA；采用高斯过程建模接收器运动路径，并使用双重随机变分推断进行轨迹重建。

Result: 在180uW功耗下实现约15m精度，增加RSS测量后可在600uW下达到约10m精度；成功追踪38mg（含电源）的移动接收器，并应用于大黄蜂回巢飞行实测。

Conclusion: 该RSS最小测量与概率推理方法可显著降低功耗与硬件负担，为超轻型移动目标的长期野外追踪提供可行方案，尤其在飞行昆虫行为学研究中具实用价值。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [158] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出利用图神经网络解决归纳式相关聚类问题，实现对新图实例的高效泛化，推理时间降低5个数量级且近似比接近最优基线，并可作为可学习池化机制提升GNN图分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相关聚类算法存在扩展性差且本质上是直推式的，即每个新问题都需要从头计算，无法处理未见过的图实例。

Method: 将相关聚类问题归纳化，训练GNN学习常见结构模式和节点特征，从而快速泛化到同分布的新图上，同时结合近似算法确保聚类质量。

Result: 在归纳设置下推理时间最多降低5个数量级，近似比保持在最佳基线解的10%以内；在标准直推式基准上也具有竞争力，并作为池化层增强了GNN捕获层次结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架高效且可扩展，不仅解决了相关聚类的归纳泛化问题，还展示了作为通用图池化机制的潜力，有助于GNN学习层次化结构信息。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [159] [C-Unseen: Weak Signal Detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26870)
*Yassir Lairgi,Ludovic Moncla,Khalid Benabdeslem,Rémy Cazabet,Pierre Cléau*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出C-Unseen框架，利用大语言模型在动态时序知识图谱中提取稀有且语义连贯的子图，并追踪其跨时间步的持续性来检测弱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱信号检测方法依赖关键词频率、主题模型或未类型化图拓扑，无法捕捉弱信号赖以显现的语义与关系结构。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：稀有子图提取器，使用LLM通过链式思维识别与快照主导叙事相悖的子图；弱信号警报器，追踪这些稀有子图在连续时间步中的持续性以筛选真正弱信号。

Result: 实验表明C-Unseen在性能上优于基于关键词、主题和图的基线方法。

Conclusion: C-Unseen为弱信号检测提供了一种自可解释且有效的新途径，利用语义关系和时序持续性克服传统方法局限。

Abstract: Weak signals are early, low-visibility indicators that precede significant changes before those changes become established. Existing detection methods, based on keyword frequency, topic modeling, or untyped graph topology, fail to capture the semantic and relational structure through which such signals manifest. In this paper, we propose C-Unseen, a self-interpretable framework for weak signal detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs (DTKGs). We define a weak signal as a rare, semantically coherent subgraph that proliferates across consecutive TKG snapshots. The framework operates through two modules: a Rare Subgraphs Extractor, in which an LLM identifies subgraphs whose content is in tension with the dominant snapshot narrative via chain-of-thought reasoning, and a Weak Signal Alerter, in which the persistence of these rare subgraphs is tracked across time steps to isolate true weak signals. Experimental results demonstrate that C-Unseen outperforms keyword-, topic-, and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出规划扩散策略优化（PDPO）框架，将扩散策略与PPO结合，生成短时程动作块用于密集人群导航，并引入边界约束设置以消除评估伪影，显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法每步仅输出单一反应性动作，难以表示多样化的短期避障策略；且常见基准存在无边界约束的评估伪影，导致智能体可绕过人群。

Method: PDPO先离线模仿碰撞避免演示预训练扩散策略，再用PPO在线微调（将去噪过程视为内部决策），执行时生成五步动作块并按滚动时域方式应用；同时将边界违规视为碰撞以修正评估设置。

Result: 在修改后的有界基准及原始基准上，PDPO相比强基线获得更高成功率；消融实验表明动作块在修正后的有界环境下尤为关键。

Conclusion: 动作块生成与边界约束处理共同提升密集人群导航的安全性与效率，PDPO为鲁棒机器人导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [161] [A Table Is Worth 64 Tokens: Pixel-level Compression for Multi-Table Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26949)
*Iñigo Alonso,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究针对像素级表格压缩在文档问答中的效果，发现下采样损害可读性但保留相关性信号，据此提出两步方法（先定位后推理），在长文档上节省41%令牌并提升7个准确率点，展示了高效压缩与推理的可行结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档问答常需处理长输入和表格，光学压缩虽减少令牌但影响未知，且现有方法未区分表格的可读性和相关性识别，亟需系统评估与优化策略。

Method: 系统评估五种VLM在多种压缩预算下的表现，并设计训练无关的两步流程：利用高度压缩图像识别相关表格，再对选中的表格以原生分辨率进行问答推理。

Result: 原生分辨率表格与文本等价；下采样引发推理质量下降和成本增加；高度压缩仍能有效识别相关性。两步法在长文档上实现41%令牌节省和7点准确率提升，同时比单步压缩配置减少15%令牌。

Conclusion: 通过利用表格压缩中相关性识别与可读性需求的不对称性，可以同时优化令牌效率和问答精度，为长文档表格问答提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Answering questions over real-world documents requires processing long inputs that interleave text with tables. Optical context compression, which represents context as images, promises to reduce token cost, but its effect on table understanding remains unclear. We study pixel-level table compression for question answering over documents with multiple tables, evaluating five VLMs across two benchmarks and five visual-token budgets. Representing tables as images at native resolution matches text in both performance and efficiency, but downscaling them makes models compensate the loss in readability with longer, less effective reasoning traces that cancel the expected savings. Highly downscaled tables, however, preserve enough signal to identify whether they are relevant to a question. We exploit this asymmetry with a training-free, two-step method: the model first identifies the tables needed to answer a question from a pixel-compressed context, and then reasons over those at native resolution. On long documents, our method saves 41% of total tokens and gains 7 accuracy points over single-step QA with native resolution tables. It also uses 15% fewer tokens than the most efficient single-step compressed configuration, with no accuracy loss.

</details>


### [162] [From Atomic to Agentic: Towards Interpretable Evaluation of LLMs' Agentic Mathematical Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26950)
*Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Yunze Song,Keyu Chen,Zhifeng Shen,Yangning Li,Yidong Wang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Kai Jin,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种面向LLM智能体数学推理的过程级基准，通过细粒度评估揭示端到端准确率相似模型在智能体能力上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准仅评估最终答案，缺乏过程级诊断价值，难以指导LLM向稳健智能体转化。

Method: 构建与可复用数学原子能力分类法对齐的智能体行为框架，设计规划、行动和反馈任务，覆盖文本与多模态场景，并通过受控LLM重写自动合成高质量轨迹和细粒度标注。

Result: 发现端到端准确率相近的模型可能具有显著不同的智能体能力画像。

Conclusion: 过程级评估对解读LLM真实潜力及指导下一代数学智能体开发至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are evolving from performing end-to-end mathematical reasoning to integrating agentic intelligence. However, most existing math benchmarks evaluate only final answers. This outcome-oriented evaluation provides limited diagnostic value for identifying process-level failures or rigorous logic, failing to guide the transformation of LLMs into robust agents. To bridge this gap, we present a process-level benchmark designed to evaluate the inherent agentic mathematical reasoning abilities of LLMs. Our framework aligns problem-solving agentic behaviors with a structured taxonomy of reusable mathematical atomic capabilities. We design a comprehensive suite of planning, action, and feedback tasks across both textual and multimodal contexts, supported by an automated pipeline that synthesizes high-quality trajectories and produces fine-grained annotations via controlled LLM rewriting. Experiments reveal that models with similar end-to-end accuracy can exhibit markedly different agentic capability profiles. This demonstrates that process-level evaluation is crucial for interpreting the true potential of LLMs and guiding the development of next-generation mathematical agents.

</details>


### [163] [GraphMemix: Query-Aware Evidence Forests for Long-Term Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26983)
*Geng Li,Yuhao Wang,Dong Li,Jianye Hao,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GraphMemix，一种基于组合优化的图记忆框架，通过查询感知的证据森林构建来组织多模态长期记忆，在提升准确率的同时显著降低生命周期成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆方法存在两类问题：离线摘要方法昂贵且与查询无关，而朴素嵌入相似度匹配会引入不完整和冗余的上下文。

Method: 包含三个组件：候选图构建（通过模式与语义关系扩展多视角种子记忆）、证据效用与激活成本（解耦直接记忆支持与锚定条件关系验证以抑制冗余/冲突信息）、森林优化（在最大证据预算下联合选择森林格式记忆上下文及其可靠关系结构）。

Result: 在四个长期多模态记忆基准上，使用不同基础模型均取得显著改进，并建立了准确率与生命周期成本之间的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过将记忆组织为查询相关子图，GraphMemix避免了昂贵的全生命周期处理，并能恢复低相似度的互补证据，为多模态长期记忆管理提供了更高效且有效的方案。

Abstract: Organizing long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging because existing methods either suffer from expensive question-agnostic offline summaries or naive embedding similarity matching that introduces incomplete and redundant context. To address these issues, we propose GraphMemix, a combinatorial-optimization graph memory framework that models memory organization as query-aware evidence-forest construction. Specifically, our method consists of three key components:(1) candidate graph construction, which expands multi-view seed memories through schema and semantic relations to acquire query-aware original context; (2) evidence utility and activation costs, which decouples direct memory support from anchor-conditioned relation verification to suppress redundant or conflicting information; and (3) forest optimization, which jointly selects a forest-format memory context under a maximum evidence budget and its reliable relational structure. By organizing memory into a query-relevant subgraph, the method avoids substantial lifecycle cost and recovers low-similarity complementary evidence. Experimental results across four long-term multimodal memory benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements with different foundation models and establish a new Pareto frontier between accuracy and lifecycle cost.

</details>


### [164] [DSA: Evidence-Aware LLM-Agent Orchestration for Multi-Market Stock Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26990)
*Linsen Zhu,Yi Shi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: DSA是一个面向多市场股票研究的证据感知编排框架，通过工作流组织、模型路由和风险控制，提升LLM代理在金融研究中的实施一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在金融信息摘要方面表现良好，但缺乏对异构证据的整合、缺失数据的暴露及生成观点对最终报告影响的控制，难以直接构建可操作的股票研究系统。

Method: DSA将工作流划分为证据采集、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选的角色与策略技能推理，以及带选择性上下文和诊断的报告生成；默认配置与代理配置共享证据和路由服务，但采用特定验证与风险保障；代理配置中角色输出经解析器处理，策略技能意见经信号资格划分后合成，分歧明确提供给决策代理并叠加保守风险覆盖。

Result: 在冻结软件快照上，1,457个可移植离线后端合同测试全部通过，其中596个案例追溯映射到六个合同族，验证了实现与LLM代理架构的一致性，而非报告质量、预测精度或投资回报的优越性。

Conclusion: DSA通过合同测试证明了其软件实现的合规性，但研究明确不声称其能提升报告质量或投资业绩，强调其价值在于系统化证据处理与风险控制框架。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [165] [ASIL: Replacing Screenshot-and-Click with Structured State and Semantic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26991)
*Rui Xie,Lu Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ASIL（代理-软件交互层），以结构化JSON观测和可执行语义动作替代截图点击，提升软件操作代理在15个应用和380个任务上的效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截图点击界面状态不完整、动作脆弱且语义弱，不匹配长程规划，限制代理操作GUI软件的能力。

Method: 为每个应用构建最深的可行访问路径，暴露为结构化JSON观测和代码可执行的语义动作，并支持训练。

Result: ASIL在闭源模型上成功率超80%，平均动作少于5次；截图点击在50步预算下成功率仅6.6/26.6，对比原生接口超UNO API 28-38点，SFT和RL进一步提升小模型性能。

Conclusion: ASIL为软件操作代理提供更高效、可训练的交互范式，优于截图点击并接近或超过部分原生API。

Abstract: Powerful code agents can execute scripts, call tools, and manage files, yet many important applications remain accessible primarily through graphical user interfaces. We argue that screenshot-and-click is an inefficient interface for software-operating agents: screenshots are state-incomplete, and GUI actions are brittle, semantically weak, and poorly matched to long-horizon planning. We introduce ASIL (Agent-Software Interaction Layer), an agent-native interface that exposes software through structured JSON observations and code-executable semantic actions, realized through the deepest feasible access path for each application. We instantiate ASIL across 15 applications and a benchmark of 300 single-application and 80 multi-application tasks. ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models while executing fewer than five actions per task. Under a repaired runtime and a 50-step screenshot budget, the same tasks yield 6.6 and 26.6 strict success under screenshot-and-click control, rising to 15.0 and 53.3 on an easier OSWorld-comparable band. Against application-native interfaces on matched tasks, ASIL exceeds LibreOffice's UNO API by 28-38 strict points but only matches draw.io's MCP content contract. The structured modality also suits training: small-scale SFT raises Qwen3.5-2B from 58.0 to 72.1 and Qwen3.5-9B from 66.6 to 80.4, and resource-limited on-policy RL further raises them to 74.4 and 82.2.

</details>


### [166] [A Multi-Modal AI Framework for Real-Time Queue Prediction, Management and Optimisation in Intelligent Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27010)
*Varvara Mama,Eleni Veroni,Nikolaos Kapsalis,Christos D. Nikolopoulos,Anargyros T. Baklezos*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合LSTM预测、MPC控制和调度优化的多模态AI框架，用于边境检查站的动态排队管理，在合成数据上相比ARIMA和规则方法，预测误差降低35%，等待时间减少30%，吞吐量提升20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边境排队管理系统依赖静态数据，无法应对动态交通和不确定性，导致性能欠佳，因此需要一种能实时适应流量变化并优化资源分配的方案。

Method: 构建多模态AI框架，集成异构数据源并用统一表示呈现，使用LSTM网络进行队列预测，结合模型预测控制（MPC）和调度优化生成可执行的控制策略，供边境官员使用。

Result: 在合成流量数据上评估显示，队列预测误差最多降低35%，平均等待时间减少30%，平均吞吐量提升近20%，均优于ARIMA和基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 将AI架构与优化技术结合能有效实现主动、自适应的边境交通管理，证明该方法的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: In the present work an efficient border control management procedure is proposed. Compared to operational queue management systems, whose operations are based on mostly static data, the proposed work takes into account dynamic traffic conditions, thus enabling optimal performance, even in cases of uncertainty. To this end, we are proposing a multi-modal Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework, tailored to th needs of border control systems, which enables real-time queue prediction, management, and resource optimization. The novel proposed approach integrates heterogeneous data sources and presents them through a unified representation by employing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for queue forecasting. Furthermore, it leverages Model Predictive Control (MPC) and scheduling optimization to derive actionable control policies, which in turn can be presented to border control officers. The proposed work has been evaluated using synthetic data simulating realistic traffic. The evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces queue prediction error by up to 35% and average waiting time by 30%. Accordingly, the average throughput increases by nearly 20%, compared to ARIMA and rule-based methods. The abovementioned results show the effectiveness and efficiency of combining AI architectures with optimization techniques for proactive and adaptive border traffic management.

</details>


### [167] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）在LLM推理后训练中的优化行为，发现ES相比GRPO能产生更广泛的推理覆盖，在保持Pass@1的同时提升Pass@K，且参数漂移虽大但功能稀疏，不必然导致灾难性遗忘，并揭示了超参数与模型规模的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES虽作为内存高效的后训练范式兴起，但其优化行为未被充分研究，难以界定其相对于GRPO等主流范式的优势范围。

Method: 通过理论分析（verifier投影的JS散度与Pass@K的关系）和系统实验对比ES与GRPO的动态机制，提出顺序GRPO-ES训练策略，并分析参数漂移的稀疏性及超参数影响。

Result: ES理论上和实证上均优于GRPO的推理覆盖，GRPO出现熵塌缩而ES同时提升Pass@1和Pass@K；顺序训练策略结合两者优势；ES的绩效增益仅来自少量大幅更新，且不导致灾难性遗忘；更大LLM需要更小种群规模。

Conclusion: ES应被视为一种独立的推理后训练范式，而非GRPO的低效内存节省替代品。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


### [168] [pro-team at LLMs4OL 2026 Tasks Flagship and Reuse: Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Vocabulary-Constrained Filtering for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27101)
*Shivam Mishra,Dhannu Ram Meena,Muneendra Ojha,Krishna Pratap Singh,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个离线检索增强少样本提示流水线，使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct和all-MiniLM-L6-v2，在LLMs4OL 2026挑战中任务B显著优于任务A。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在从文本中学习本体时会出现幻觉、格式不一致和偏好层级关系等问题；因此需要一个离线检索增强并能同时完成端到端旗舰任务和本体扩展复用任务的系统。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct的少样本提示，用all-MiniLM-L6-v2检索示例（任务A取top-5，任务B取top-2），并对长提示使用左截断的上下文窗口；任务B生成的三元组经确定性词汇约束过滤，保留至少一个端点在样本词汇内的三元组并去除初始本体重复。

Result: 任务B获得语义图相似度0.8692、术语类型F1 0.9200、分类学发现F1 0.8540；任务A获得语义图相似度0.7416；但未提取到非分类关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明检索增强提示有效，但当前封闭的、面向分类的关系词汇限制了非分类关系提取，需改进关系词汇设计以覆盖更丰富的语义关系。

Abstract: Ontology learning from text remains challenging despite significant progress in Large Language Models (LLMs), which can hallucinate domain terms, produce inconsistent formats, and favor hierarchical over associative relations. In the LLMs4OL 2026 Challenge, we address both the End-to-End Flagship Task (Task A) and Ontology Extension Reuse Task (Task B) using an offline retrieval-augmented few-shot prompting pipeline. Our system employs Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 for demonstration retrieval, selecting the top-5 examples for Task A and top-2 for Task B. A left-truncated context-windowing strategy preserves task instructions within long prompts. For Task B, generated triples undergo deterministic vocabulary-constrained filtering, retaining triples when at least one endpoint belongs to the sample's closed term/type vocabulary and removing duplicates of the initial ontology. The approach achieves Semantic Graph Similarity of 0.8692, Term-Typing F1 of 0.9200, and Taxonomy Discovery F1 of 0.8540 on Task B, while Task A achieves 0.7416 Semantic Graph Similarity. However, no non-taxonomic relations are extracted, highlighting limitations of closed, taxonomy-oriented relation vocabularies.

</details>


### [169] [When Tool Outputs Become Commands: Separating Action Induction from Runtime Authorization in Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27146)
*Xiaokun Guo,Zhen Xu,Dongdong Huo,Yanqiu Zhang,Wei Wang,Qinfu Yang,Dongjin Yu,Yu Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SARA框架，将动作诱导与执行授权分离，通过环境隔离的动作探针和证据审计，将攻击成功率（ASR）控制在0.63%以下，同时保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的LLM智能体依赖不可信的运行观测，但观测不仅提供数据，还可能包含具体动作指令，导致副作用超出用户意图。核心风险在于混淆了动作诱导与执行授权，需明确区分二者。

Method: SARA将动作诱导与执行授权设为独立运行时角色：在观测侧，用上下文隔离的动作探针暴露诱导语义并记录来源，作为审计信号；在执行侧，仅当工具调用满足用户目标、执行链及参数级支持时，才根据授权证据执行。同时引入无历史晋升机制，防止历史重复将动作来源提升为执行权限。

Result: 在AgentDojo和AgentDyn四个主要评估设置中，SARA将ASR限制在≤0.63%，并保持竞争力的任务效用，且在多个智能体骨干上持续降低ASR。

Conclusion: SARA有效解决了工具输出中动作指令带来的安全风险，通过分离机制显著抑制攻击成功率，同时兼顾任务效果，为安全工具增强LLM智能体提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents must rely on untrusted runtime Observations to complete open-ended tasks; however, when tool outputs no longer merely provide data but begin to specify concrete actions, they effectively become ``commands'' that can drive real-world side effects beyond user intent. We argue that this risk arises from conflating action induction with execution authorization. To address this distinction, we propose SARA, which treats action induction and execution authorization as distinct runtime roles and separates action provenance from execution authority. On the Observation side, a context-isolated Action Probe exposes action-inducing semantics and persistently records action-origin provenance across steps as a review signal; on the execution side, actual tool calls are authorized only against the user objective and audited evidence from authorized successful executions, while satisfying goal, execution-chain, and argument-level support. To preserve this separation across multi-step execution, SARA applies No-History-Promotion to prevent historical recurrence from laundering action origins into execution authority. Across AgentDojo and AgentDyn, SARA limits ASR to no more than \(0.63\%\) across four primary evaluation settings while maintaining competitive task utility, and consistently reduces ASR across additional Agent backbones.

</details>


### [170] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: LLM智能体在面对包装精美的市场面板时，对不可预测问题的方向性承诺从6.5%飙升至54.0%，即使面板数据完全虚构，承诺率仍与真实数据无显著差异；问题不在能力或信念，而在行为决策门控的失效，且该门控可通过微调修复但高度依赖上下文格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何LLM会在无信息含量的问题上做出自信的方向性判断，并定位失效环节（认知、信念、判断还是行动门控），以揭示表面权威性包装对模型决策的影响。

Method: 对12个前沿模型进行受控实验，逐步升级市场面板证据（包括完全虚构数据），测量模型的承诺率、陈述概率及可知道性判断；并通过对3B模型在540个合成案例（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器）上的监督微调，测试门控的可训练性。

Result: 承诺率从裸问题的6.5%升至完整面板的54.0%；虚构面板仍达36.8%，与真实数据（37.6%）无显著差异；陈述概率变化极小且劣于基线；模型能90%识别不可知但仅在0.4%此类情况拒绝行动；微调将原始案例承诺率降至0.0%并泛化到三个全新领域，但在严格格式（无推理空间）下失效。

Conclusion: 行动门控是可训练且上下文脆弱的独立模块；部署时需同时考虑其可修复性及对响应格式的敏感性，否则模型会在本可正确回答的问题上因格式压迫而自信犯错。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [171] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级单线提示优化方法NPO，仅通过教师模型迭代修订提示，即可在更少采样下达到或超越复杂优化器GEPA的性能，且优势随教师模型增强而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法日益复杂，但缺乏对简单方法潜力的充分探索；同时需要降低递归自我改进中智能体优化的计算成本，并验证强模型推理能否替代复杂搜索。

Method: NPO采用单线迭代框架，每次迭代由教师模型基于当前提示和 rollout 反馈生成修订版本，无需并行搜索或多候选评估，极大简化优化过程。

Result: 在多个任务上NPO较GEPA用更少rollout取得相当或更优结果；教师模型越强，NPO相对优势越明显；在交互式游戏中整体与GEPA持平，GRPO在部分任务上更优；NPO优化提示可迁移至同系列其他学生模型。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，简单的线性提示优化可媲美甚至超越复杂搜索方法，为递归自我改进提供高效且可扩展的替代方案，并暗示模型推理能力与搜索复杂度之间存在互补性。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [172] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CorporateBench基准，包含超过23万文档的企业级语料，用于评估LLM在真实规模下的信息抽取与知识库查询能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业文档QA评测缺乏真实规模数据：真实数据涉及隐私，合成数据过于简单，无法反映LLM在实际企业通信网络中的表现。

Method: 基于时间演化的知识库生成四个从12到10000员工规模的合成公司，保证跨文档逻辑一致性，构建多任务Q&A基准CB。

Result: 评测五个LLM发现，随输入规模接近实际场景，模型性能显著下降。

Conclusion: CB填补了企业通信推理基准的空白，为LLM开发者提供关键性能指标。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出关系超图变换器（RHT），用超图和五维嵌入处理关系数据库，注意力复杂度与平均关系度成比例，在Synthea基准上展现强嵌入语义连贯性和计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗等多表学习面临数据量大、高维、高基数类别、表间依赖和时序重复观测等复杂挑战，现有方法难以统一高效处理。

Method: RHT将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），执行稀疏关系注意力，复杂度为O(平均关系度×实体数)，并提供开源实现。

Result: 在Synthea电子健康记录多标签预测任务中，RHT取得最强嵌入语义连贯性，计算可扩展；XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上最高；消融实验验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: RHT为关系学习提供统一可扩展方案，语义嵌入优势显著，未来将进行MIMIC-IV临床验证；罕见标签性能仍需改进。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [174] [Thresholding Post-Quantum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26792)
*Francesco De Sclavis,Matteo Nardelli,Marco Pedicini*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于多种后量子密码范式的门限签名方案，并提出了按范式分类的构建工具分类法，同时涵盖FHE、MPC和ZKP等通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门限签名应用主要依赖预量子签名，虽高效但在后量子时代不安全。NIST的呼吁和实际应用需求促使系统研究后量子门限签名，但缺乏统一的构建工具分类和范式比较。

Method: 按密码学范式（格问题、单向哈希函数、密码群作用、同源、多变量系统）对现有后量子签名进行归类，并梳理用于构建T/N门限方案的通用工具（如FHE、MPC、ZKP）。

Result: 给出了基于不同范式的门限签名构建工具分类框架，覆盖了主要后量子签名类型，并整合了通用技术路线。

Conclusion: 为后量子门限签名的研究与标准化提供了结构化参考，有助于指导未来方案设计与选择。

Abstract: Threshold signature schemes distribute the signing process among $T$ parties out of $N$. They enable a variety of applications and their research is also motivated by a recent NIST call. However, applications are dominated by pre-quantum signatures, which are more efficient but not secure in the post-quantum setting. This paper investigates existing post-quantum signatures, based on a variety of paradigms: lattice problems, one-way (hash) functions, cryptographic group actions, isogenies and multivariate systems. We propose a classification (divided by paradigm) of existing tools that are used to build $T$-out-of-$N$ schemes from digital signatures. We also include general approaches based on FHE, MPC or ZKP.

</details>


### [175] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedCMAPSS，一个基于 NASA C-MAPSS 数据集的联邦学习剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准，包含五个标准化任务以模拟从独立同分布到极端统计异质性的工业场景，并系统评估多种联邦优化算法和神经网络架构，公开代码和数据划分以建立可复现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的预测性维护在工业 4.0 中至关重要，但 RUL 估计模型的训练常受限于全生命周期失效数据稀缺；联邦学习虽能解决数据隐私与共享问题，却缺乏统一的评估框架来公平比较不同方法，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 基于 C-MAPSS 数据集构建 FedCMAPSS 基准，设计五个标准化任务覆盖理想 IID 设置至极端统计异质性，并针对多种神经网络架构系统评估最先进的联邦优化算法，同时公开源代码与数据划分以保证可复现性。

Result: 通过系统实验建立了可复现的基线结果，展示了不同联邦算法与架构在异质性任务下的性能差异，为联邦预测性维护提供了标准化的比较基础。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS 为联邦 RUL 估计提供了标准评估框架，填补了该领域缺乏公共基准的空白，旨在推动联邦预测性维护解决方案的开发与公平对比。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [176] [Information Flow Control in Off-Chain Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26858)
*Stian Lybech,Eun-Young Kang,Riccardo Tonello,Anders Dalskov*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文为带链下组件的区块链智能合约语言建立形式化模型，并利用静态信息流控制分析链上-链下数据完整性与保密性，发现即使无循环结构，链下组件仍可通过递归调用编码阻塞构造导致信息流控制失效，最后讨论补救方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链下组件虽提升智能合约灵活性（如访问外部数据、跨链交互），但也引入新安全漏洞；现有模型未系统刻画链下执行与链上状态同步带来的信息流风险，需形式化研究其保密性与完整性保证。

Method: 构建包含链上与链下组件的智能合约语言操作语义模型；将链下组件视为独立线程；采用静态信息流控制（如依赖分析/类型系统）检验数据保密性与完整性属性，并通过反例构造证明其不可判定或失效。

Result: 即使语言不含循环构造，链下组件的独立线程特性可通过递归方法调用实现阻塞式交互，从而使简单静态信息流控制失效，无法同时保证数据完整性与保密性。

Conclusion: 现有静态信息流控制技术不足以应对带链下组件的区块链合约安全，需探索更细粒度并发分析、时序逻辑或动态机制等补救途径；研究为未来安全链下合约设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a model of a smart-contract language for a blockchain architecture with off-chain components. Off-chain components are pieces of smart contracts that execute at designated locations outside of the network of blockchain nodes, but remain synchronised with the on-chain contract state. They react to changes to the on-chain state, but may also notify the on-chain component about events in the world, e.g. stock prices, weather data etc., or even act as a bridge between different blockchains. This affords greater flexibility for the developer, but may also enable new vulnerabilities. As a concrete example, we use the model to study the problem of ensuring integrity and secrecy of data between the on-chain and off-chain components, using static information flow control techniques. This fails, even in the absence of a loop construct, because off-chain components act as separate threads and can encode a blocking construct e.g. through recursive method calls. We end the paper with a discussion of possible ways to remedy this situation.

</details>


### [177] [Metamorphism: A mathematical challenge for antivirus technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27007)
*Luis M. Augusto*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出利用van Wijngaarden文法实现病毒变形，并指出杀毒软件需嵌入图灵机以判定该文法生成的语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形病毒能自我变异为无限多个功能相同但代码高度不同的副本，现有杀毒软件几乎无法检测，因此需要探索新的理论方法应对。

Method: 采用van Wijngaarden文法模拟病毒变形机制，通过文法生成规则实现代码的无限变异。

Result: 证明病毒变形可由该文法完全实现，但检测难度等价于让杀毒软件判定文法生成语言，即需嵌入图灵机。

Conclusion: 变形病毒在理论上可构造且检测极具挑战，杀毒软件必须具有图灵完备的判定能力才能应对。

Abstract: Metamorphic viruses, currently the most advanced computer viruses in the wild, have the unique ability of mutating their own code virtually into infinitely many highly dissimilar copies of themselves that nevertheless have the same functionality. This ability -- metamorphism -- together with other advanced ob- fuscation techniques makes these computer viruses virtually undetectable by the antivirus software available in the market today. In this paper, we show that viral metamorphism is fully attainable by the employment of van Wijngaarden grammars. The challenge then for an antivirus software is to embed the Turing machine that decides the language generated by the grammar.

</details>


### [178] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出将‘可定量性’作为高通量筛选中的独立分流目标，证明其可从低成本筛选特征预测，并据此优化剂量反应实验资源的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统筛选策略只关注生物活性确认，忽略了确认活性后仍可能无法获得可报告剂量反应估计的问题，导致昂贵实验资源浪费。

Method: 构建定量性预测框架，对比筛选特征与分子结构的预测贡献，并在未见化学骨架和不同检测机制家族上评估响应特征预测的稳健性。

Result: 定量性可被强预测，响应特征为主要信息源，且对全新化学骨架和检测机制泛化良好；成功定量概率随响应幅度和检测情境变化显著。

Conclusion: 实验可测量性是独立于生物活性的可预测属性，定量性感知分流能显著提高剂量反应实验资源的使用效率。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [179] [Can You Say This for Me? Speaking Up by Proxy in Co-Located Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26185)
*Yue Shen,Rehema Abulikemu,Ryan P. McMahan,Yan Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: SecondVoice是一个混合现实系统，通过具身虚拟代理让用户在集体讨论中匿名发声，将观点与发言者分离，从而降低社交风险，促进平等参与。初步研究显示，相比匿名文本板，它更能鼓励用户表达原本不敢说出口的观点，并引发多轮小组讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对面协作中，人们常因担心负面人际或职业后果而抑制发言，尤其是需要自己开口时。现有匿名渠道（如文本板）缺乏互动性，且发言易被忽略，因此需要一种既能保护发言者身份又能让观点进入实时口语讨论的机制。

Method: 系统采用混合现实私密覆盖层，用户通过结构化流程指定意图而非完整语句，系统重述后通过虚拟代理将观点以语音形式投入讨论。研究设计了一个社交风险下的参与渠道设计空间，并在N=16的受试者内研究中，将完整系统与匿名文本板进行对比，覆盖两个小组讨论任务。

Result: 50%的参与者使用SecondVoice表达了未说出口的观点，而文本板仅为18.8%。代理传递的观点进入口语讨论并引发多轮小组互动，而文本板帖子未出现此类现象。参与者认为该渠道在特定情境下有价值，但也指出时机、所有权和重述信任度方面的权衡。

Conclusion: SecondVoice证明了分离发言内容与发言者身份是降低社交风险、促进平等参与的有效方式，但其实际应用需权衡时机控制、观点归属感及系统重述的可信度，未来可进一步优化结构化输入和代理表达方式。

Abstract: Equal participation in co-located discussion is important for effective collaboration, yet people often hold back when they anticipate negative interpersonal or professional consequences, especially when raising a point requires voicing it themselves. We present SecondVoice, a mixed-reality system that enables people to speak up through an embodied virtual proxy. By separating what is said from who says it, SecondVoice brings hesitant points into the live spoken discussion without putting the speaker on the spot. Using a private overlay, users specify their intent through a structured specification process rather than composing a full utterance. The system reformulates the input and voices it into the conversation through the proxy. We characterize a design space of participation channels under social risk. In a preliminary within-subject study (N = 16), we compare the complete SecondVoice system with an anonymous text-board channel across two group discussion tasks. Half of participants reported using SecondVoice for a point they did not say aloud, compared with 18.8% for the text board. Proxy-delivered points entered the spoken floor and were followed by multi-turn group engagement, which we did not observe after text-board posts. Participants described the channel as situationally valuable but identified tradeoffs around timing, ownership, and trust in reformulation.

</details>


### [180] [Invocation-Level Reliability of Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26189)
*Afiya Noorain,Subhranshu Mohanty,Amritesh Banerjee,Abhijit Dasgupta*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 工具使用智能体在深度6时约70%的能力因自身早期错误而丧失；精确匹配评分规则固定了传播模型的严重性和恢复参数，导致严重性被压至边界、恢复性结构上不可观测；条件状态评分可解绑参数，恢复内部估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法未区分工具选择错误与参数构造错误，且精确匹配评分在自回归传播中产生系统性偏差，掩盖真实能力损耗。

Method: 在深度1-8的无污染多步任务上，对五个开放权重模型分别测量教师强制上下文和自回归上下文中的正确调用率，并对比精确匹配与条件状态评分下参数估计的差异。

Result: 精确匹配下严重性参数被强制为0（869个中毒步骤全错），恢复参数不可观测（580个中毒步骤0次恢复，随机期望为0.0058）；拟合仍给出0.92和0.73的置信值，但真实值为1.000；条件状态评分将严重性估计从0提升至+0.149和+0.316。

Conclusion: 固定金轨迹的精确匹配评分使传播参数不可估计，需改用条件状态评分以正确衡量工具使用智能体的真实鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tool-using agents fail two ways: choosing the wrong tool, or forming wrong arguments, and an early failure of either kind can silently corrupt everything downstream. We measure a correct-invocation rate that separates the two, under both a clean teacher-forced context and the model's own free-running context, on five open-weight models over contamination-free multi-step tasks (depths 1-8). By depth 6, roughly 70% of a model's own clean-context capability is lost to its own earlier mistakes (L6 = 0.686, 0.684). Our central finding concerns the measurement itself. Under exact-match scoring against a fixed gold trajectory, a propagation model's severity and recovery parameters are not merely hard to estimate - they are fixed by the scoring rule. Severity is forced to its boundary (0 of 869 poisoned steps correct); recovery is structurally unobservable (0 of 580 poisoned steps returned on-track, against an expected 0.0058 by chance). Both follow from one mechanism: post-divergence, the gold value is generated by tool constants the model never sees, so it is information the model cannot derive. A fit run anyway returns 0.92 and 0.73 for a quantity that is exactly 1.000 - confident numbers for a parameter the scoring rule already determined. We give the mechanism and a remedy, conditional-on-state scoring, applied retrospectively to cached completions at zero additional cost, which un-pins severity to interior estimates excluding zero (+0.149, +0.316).

</details>


### [181] [Structured Evidence Routing for Incident Risk Prediction from Multimodal Longitudinal EHRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26191)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出结构化证据路由方法，通过路由器-预测器-审查器流程处理纵向电子健康记录，在不牺牲性能的前提下提供患者特异的证据链，在五项一年期疾病发病预测任务上达到与监督基线相当的AUROC和AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向EHR中的发病风险预测具有挑战性，因为相关信号是多模态、孤立微弱且分布在不规则的患者历史中，现有方法难以有效整合这些信号并生成可解释的预测。

Method: 采用路由器-预测器-审查器工作流：路由器将完整索引前EHR组织成紧凑摘要和针对性证据切片，预测器基于证据生成风险评估，审查器进行批判性修正；同时，为与监督基线比较，将路由证据摘要与监督分类器读出配对。

Result: 在五项一年期发病诊断任务上，方法达到与成熟监督EHRSHOT基线相当的AUROC范围，并在AUPRC上保持竞争力，同时暴露患者特异的证据轨迹；内部消融显示路由、实验室证据、任务引导和审查均对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化证据路由能在保持预测性能的同时提供可解释的证据链，各组件均有效，为临床决策支持提供了透明且可靠的发病风险预测框架。

Abstract: Incident risk prediction from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) is challenging because relevant signals are multimodal, weak in isolation, and distributed across irregular patient histories. We propose structured evidence routing, a router-predictor-reviewer workflow that separates full-record access from disease-specific assessment. The router organizes the complete pre-index EHR into a compact summary and targeted evidence slices; the predictor uses this evidence to form an evidence-linked risk assessment, which the reviewer critiques. For comparison with supervised EHRSHOT baselines, we pair the routed evidence summaries with a supervised classifier readout. Across five 1-year incident diagnosis tasks, our method reaches the AUROC range of established supervised EHRSHOT baselines and remains competitive on AUPRC, while exposing a patient-specific evidence trail. Internal pre-readout ablations further suggest that routing, laboratory evidence, task guidance, and review each contribute to performance.

</details>


### [182] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究在多种互补可再生能源场景下，系统对比了传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习方法。结果显示：Extra Trees在结构化WEC数据上最优（较MLP降低63.7% MAE），STGCN在时空建模中达R=0.93，RF-BiLSTM混合模型整体最佳（MAE=150.5 kW），且无单一架构普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源功率预测中，不同AI架构在不同数据形态（结构化布局、时空序列、表格与动态混合）下的适用性缺乏系统对比，导致模型选择缺乏依据。

Method: 在三个数据集（大规模WEC、16 WEC、Penmanshiel风场SCADA）上，对七类模型进行统一基准测试，包括随机树集成（Extra Trees）、图时空网络（STGCN）及RF-BiLSTM混合模型，并采用MAE和R等指标评估。

Result: Extra Trees在WEC布局数据中MAE较MLP降低63.7%；STGCN达到MAE≈167.0 kW且R=0.93；RF-BiLSTM整体最优（MAE=150.5 kW），较LSTM降低75%，较STGCN提升10%。

Conclusion: 无单一架构通吃所有场景：树集成适合结构化代理建模，图网络适合显式空间交互，混合循环集成适合时空与表格动态共存，模型选择应基于数据特性。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [183] [6.5% of the Neuro-Symbolic Literature Can Be Reproduced from Its Published Artifacts, a Six-Stage Audit Framework and First Instantiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26236)
*Brandon Colelough,Vladimir Martirosyan,Ishan Tamrakar,William Regli,Aditya Kumar,Anh N. Nhu,Dhruv Dubey,Raj Ambavane,Haowei Deng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个六阶段可重复性审计框架，并在神经符号AI领域实施，发现仅6.52%的研究可复现，大量论文即使声明代码可用仍因缺少工件而无法复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学领域研究可重复性普遍不足，但缺乏系统性审计方法，尤其在神经符号AI子领域尚无量化评估。

Method: 构建六阶段框架：检索去重、标题摘要筛选、全文本筛选、代码工件核查与清单建立、有限复现运行、结果量化；并对1,304篇合格论文执行审计。

Result: 共复现85项研究（占合格语料6.52%，占尝试复现的18.68%）；321项因缺少非代码工件受阻，42项因代码仓库缺失或不可用受阻。

Conclusion: 现有“代码可用”声明并不可靠，应强制要求论文投稿时提供完整、版本化且永久存档的工件捆绑包，以改善可重复性。

Abstract: We present a six-stage framework for auditing the reproducibility of scientific claims across a research literature within the computer science domain, and instantiate our framework for the neuro-symbolic AI (NSAI) subdomain. Instantiating the framework on the NSAI subdomain produced a multi-year audit. Stage one retrieved 5,497 records and removed 3,018 duplicates. Stage two screened the 2,479 unique records at title and abstract, identifying 1,365 self-identified NSAI records, then removed a further 61 at full text for off-topic, non-research, no-quantitative-evaluation, or inaccessible-full-text reasons. Stage three sought a verifiable public code artifact for each of the 1,304 eligible records and found none for 849, leaving 455 to enter the artifact inventory and bounded rerun of stages four and five. We fully or partially reproduced 85 studies, 6.52% of the eligible corpus and 18.68% of attempted reruns. We found that 321 attempted reruns were blocked by missing non- code artifacts and 42 by missing or unusable code repositories. These figures quantify a persistent reproducibility deficit that survives even nominal "code available" declarations, and signal the need for enforced, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles in future NSAI publications. We argue that empirical NSAI papers should be required at submission time to provide complete, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Client Averaging: A Client-Independent Second-Order Stationary-Bias Component in Stochastic SCAFFOLD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26765)
*Yi-Ping Tang,Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了在随机Scaffold算法中，客户端平均只能抑制一阶O(γ/N)偏差，但无法消除与客户端数量无关的O(γ²)偏差项，并给出了其精确系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恒定步长分析虽已识别出O(γ/N)主导均值偏差并指出高阶偏差可能随客户端数增加而持续存在，但未在系数层面确定首个与客户端数无关的贡献项。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对一维同质客户端、全参与、固定局部步数H和有限加性梯度噪声的随机Scaffold设置，通过均匀收敛的渐近展开分析，推导出平稳分布均值偏差的显式表达式，并分解出直接控制贡献与局部轨迹二阶矩修正的间接机制。

Result: 证明了均值偏差展开式，其中客户端平均抑制了O(γ/N)项，但留下了系数为f'''(x*)σ²/(12f''(x*))·(H-1)(5H-1)/H的客户端无关O(γ²)项；数值实验验证了预测系数、随客户端数增加的持续性及联合余项。

Conclusion: 客户端平均并非消除随机Scaffold高阶均值偏差的充分手段，即使直接控制项在全局平均中路径式抵消，局部二阶矩效应仍会通过非二次曲率转化为偏差；该系数在二次目标下消失，且结果限于一维同质固定H设置。

Abstract: Existing constant-step analysis of stochastic \Scaf{} identifies a leading $O(γ/N)$ stationary mean bias and shows that higher-order bias can persist as the client count increases, but does not identify the first client-independent contribution at coefficient level. For full-participation stochastic \Scaf{} with one-dimensional homogeneous clients, fixed local-step count $H$, and bounded additive gradient noise, we prove, uniformly over $N\ge2$,
  $$ \begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}_{π_{γ,N,H}}[x]-x^\star ={}& -\frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{4f''(x^\star)^2}\fracγ{N}\\ &- \frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{12f''(x^\star)} \frac{(H-1)(5H-1)}{H}γ^2 +O_H\!\left(\frac{γ^2}{N}+γ^3\right). \end{aligned} $$
  Hence client averaging suppresses the leading $O(γ/N)$ bias but does not remove the client-independent $O(γ^2)$ component when its coefficient is nonzero.
  The mechanism is indirect: although the direct control contribution cancels pathwise in the linear global average, the controls still alter within-round local trajectories and their second moments. Fresh gradient noise and persistent control fluctuations therefore generate local second-moment corrections that nonquadratic curvature converts into stationary mean bias. The coefficient vanishes for quadratic objectives. Numerical experiments are consistent with the predicted coefficient, its persistence as client count increases, and the stated joint remainder. The result is restricted to the one-dimensional homogeneous fixed-$H$ setting.

</details>


### [185] [On the Indistinguishability of Human v/s AI Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26797)
*Jaee Ponde,Aritra Das,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究利用人类写作样本对机器生成文本进行策略性改写，使其分布逼近人类分布，并给出了收敛率与样本复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成文本与人类写作难以区分，且改写工具加剧了该问题，因此需要系统研究如何利用人类样本引导机器文本更接近人类风格。

Method: 提出多样本设置下的重复改写机制，在简单混合与稳定性条件下证明机器分布向经验人类分布收敛，并推导显式收敛率，同时扩展到有限样本情形。

Result: 得到了明确的收敛速度，量化了所需人类样本数与改写轮数随目标误差的变化关系。

Conclusion: 证明了基于人类样本的重复改写可有效缩小机器与人类文本分布差距，为检测与对抗提供理论依据。

Abstract: The rapid improvement of LLMs has made distinguishing AI-generated text from human writing a pressing problem. This challenge is further amplified by paraphrasing tools designed to make machine-generated text appear more "human". We study how access to human writing samples can be used to strategically paraphrase machine-generated responses toward the human distribution. Under a multi-sample setting with human and machine responses to the same prompts, we show that repeated paraphrasing moves the machine distribution toward the empirical human distribution under simple mixing and stability conditions. Our results derive an explicit convergence rate, extend the analysis to a finite-sample setting, and characterize how the required number of human samples and paraphrasing rounds scale with the desired error.

</details>


### [186] [Approved Too Late: Verdict Staleness in LLM-Guarded Self-Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26306)
*Ilai Shraga,Roei Eshel,Lior Gorelik*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应性系统中LLM护栏判决的时效性问题，提出Freshness-Bounded Shield方法，通过安全边际和特征波动估计判决有效期，显著降低过期风险，并定义时效契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM护栏在检查时给出的批准可能在执行时已失效，造成TOCTOU风险，现有研究缺乏对判决时效性的系统分析。

Method: 区分三种时效度量，提出FBS方法，利用安全侧边际和特征波动性估计批准有效期，无需显式动力学模型。

Result: 在五个环境中，全候选判决变化率5.3-48.4%；FBS将过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%；四个LLM法官均存在使用时效无效性。

Conclusion: 提出时效契约要求，强调判决需在检查和执行时均有效，FBS可有效缓解TOCTOU风险，但需进一步审计法官可靠性。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) guardrail for a self-adaptive system (SAS) may issue an approval that is correct at check time but stale by actuation. This creates an Execute-stage time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) hazard. We study verdict freshness: whether a guardrail verdict remains valid when used. We distinguish three quantities that answer different questions: all-candidate verdict change under fixed-action replay, oracle-labeled approval expiry on recorded closed-loop trajectories, and judge-conditioned use-time invalidity. Across five reproducible SAS environments, all-candidate verdict-change rates span 5.3-48.4% at a common replay shift of eight simulator steps. We introduce the Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS), which estimates each approval's validity horizon from its safe-side margin and recent feature volatility, without an explicit plant-dynamics model. Using fixed settings documented in the artifact, FBS reduces oracle-labeled approval-expiry rates from 3.4-24.7% to 0-1.8% at the same shift. A separate audit of four LLM judges finds nonzero judge-conditioned use-time invalidity in every approval stream. We formulate a freshness contract: every approval must be correct at check time and remain valid at use time.

</details>


### [187] [ProofEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolution for Formal Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26334)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Eric Xie,Bohan Liu,Shivani Modi,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ProofEvolve，一个神经符号框架，通过演化显式验证的证明DAG并持久化存储已验证子图，实现无需昂贵权重更新的递归自我改进，在三类Lean基准上取得最高平均求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经证明器未充分利用递归自改进结构：要么通过昂贵权重更新嵌入经验，要么仅在当前问题内保留中间推导，且依赖稀疏的全证明反馈，忽略了失败尝试中的有用发现。

Method: ProofEvolve将神经模型与符号Lean内核结合：神经模型提议变异算子（分解、修复、模式重组），Lean验证每个转换。问题内，演化带行为索引的AND-OR证明DAG存档；跨问题，提取经内核检查的模式存入持久库，通过类型化模式重组继承结果，未决前提作为新子目标。

Result: 在三个竞争级Lean基准中，ProofEvolve的平均求解率高于所有评估的证明系统。

Conclusion: 通过演化保留并重用不完整尝试中的已验证结果，ProofEvolve在不牺牲形式可靠性的前提下，有效扩展知识边界，实现更强的证明能力。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving offers a natural foundation for recursive self-improvement in scientific discovery. However, existing neural provers do not fully preserve this recursive structure, where the learning process should be self-improving over time. Existing methods either embed proof experience into model parameters through expensive weight updates, or keep verified intermediate deductions only within the current problem. In addition, these methods also heavily rely on sparse whole-proof feedback, even when unsuccessful partial attempts contain useful discoveries. To close the gap, we propose ProofEvolve, a neuro-symbolic framework that evolves explicit, formally verified symbolic proof structures with neural models to decisively expand the knowledge boundary. In this framework, the neural model proposes variation operators, including decompositions, repairs, and schema recombinations. The symbolic Lean kernel verifies every proof transition.
